## v0.340.21 private lifecycle stop worker

Application shutdown now uses one private lifecycle worker instead of the shared asyncio executor. It drains the independent emergency lane before stopping ordinary workers even when general workers are saturated. Worker-creation failure runs the same nested stop fence synchronously. The hosted regression keeps completion and ordering mandatory while allowing bounded Windows storage load beyond five seconds. This changes no Portal control, permission, retry, provider/account access, funds, live/cloud or release authority.

## v0.340.9 Windows portability and complete-history audit repair

Exact v0.340.8 source ced0884bb08acad99654042ea8a2c7f24a2678b9 failed security 40735154460, safety 40735154501 and Windows 40735154550. The separate Windows offline-evidence and rendered-browser jobs passed. Safety regression reported five backup failures after 4,353 passes/19 skips; the other Windows lane reported 596 failures/3,762 passes/19 skips, dominated by the shared file-fingerprint boundary. Packaging was skipped, so no current verified installer follows from these runs.

The backup now explicitly closes its target SQLite connection before any failure cleanup. Retained snapshot inspection uses a fresh no-follow path stat rather than Windows DirEntry metadata, whose link-count fields can be zero. The shared file fingerprint compares stable identity/size/mtime/link fields across path and handle APIs, checks creation-time stability separately inside each API, and retains path-API creation time plus the content digest for later comparisons. POSIX cross-API creation-time equality remains required. Identity, single-link, sidecar, byte-cap and changed-file refusals remain intact.

The actual reachable-history scan reproduced HISTORY_BYTE_CAP: 9,422 objects total 548,857,439 uncompressed bytes, just beyond 512 MiB. The finite default is now 1 GiB, with no ref/object omission, pruning or private-report exceptions. An incomplete audit exposes only an allowlisted reason code. The same 813-commit/5,867-blob reachable history passed the expanded scan with zero findings; current-candidate artifact and fresh exact-source security/Gitleaks checks are separate requirements.

No UI control or editable parameter is added. Preserve seven tabs, black/bright-purple bottom-right help, OFF engagement defaults, independent emergency fencing, bounded risk and exits. These fixes grant no provider, account, fee/hold/funds, routing, live/cloud or release acceptance. Rough implementation remains about 40% (30–45%, low confidence), with 0/9 fully accepted stages. The current operational priority is a green Windows build followed by authorized connected-paper acceptance and sustained genuine-market trials.

## Durable launch rules

The primary objective remains a reliable, beginner-friendly Windows Nexus Operative below 1.0 until every independent gate and explicit owner approval is satisfied. The critical path is: green Windows packaging and recovery; authenticated, bounded data observation; deterministic risk and decision handling; durable paper lifecycle and reconciliation; realistic sustained forward trials; independent safety/security review; funding/live/cloud readiness under separate governance; and explicit release approval. Passing local or hosted automation is necessary evidence, not broker acceptance, market realism, profitability, trading permission, or a launch date.

Fix current CI regressions first, then complete the largest coherent unmet release gate. Alpaca paper remains the first planned external execution service, but it stays disconnected unless an authorized paper account and private evidence volume are available. Never activate real money, move funds, procure paid services, expose credentials, bypass risk controls, or infer source/fill/fee/hold/funds acceptance from injected fixtures. Preserve OFF-by-default engagement, bounded account/risk checks, fail-closed exits, the independent emergency cut, the seven beginner tabs, and black/bright-purple bottom-right hover help for every new control or modifiable parameter.

This file intentionally keeps the current v0.340 launch window. Older sprint prose remains recoverable from Git, `ROADMAP.md`, `VERSIONING.md`, and the versioned release-trial reports; removing duplicate prose does not remove or promote any gate. Rough weighted implementation remains about 40% (30–45%, low confidence), with 0/9 fully accepted stages. Release approval and trading permission remain false.
